Results · Definitions

← suite simplipy

sp-acj4-196-real true

category simplipy-real

Summary

Given
\(x \in \mathbb{R},\quad \sin\left(\tanh x\right) \neq 1,\quad \sin\left(\tanh x\right) \neq -1,\quad \cos\left(\tanh x\right) \neq 0\)
Claim
\(\displaystyle \operatorname{artanh}\left(\sin\left(\tanh x\right)\right) = \operatorname{arsinh}\left(\tan\left(\tanh x\right)\right)\)
Expected
true equal at every admissible point

the two sides are equal at every admissible point

SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[196] + rules.json[138]: atanh sin tanh _0 -> asinh tan tanh _0

Lean proof: included

Source (real.sexp)

(case sp-acj4-196-real (category simplipy-real)
  (note "SimpliPy acj-4 (MIT) rules_real.json[196] + rules.json[138]: atanh sin tanh _0  ->  asinh tan tanh _0")
  (var x real)
  (assume (!= (sin (tanh x)) 1))
  (assume (!= (sin (tanh x)) -1))
  (assume (nonzero (cos (tanh x))))
  (ask (= (atanh (sin (tanh x))) (asinh (tan (tanh x)))))
  (expect true)
  (lean-proof "obtain ⟨r, rfl⟩ := AE.IsReal.exists hd0; have hy1 : -(Real.pi / 2) < Real.tanh r := (by linarith [Real.neg_one_lt_tanh r, Real.pi_gt_three]); have hy2 : Real.tanh r < Real.pi / 2 := (by linarith [Real.tanh_lt_one r, Real.pi_gt_three]); have ha := h0.2; have hb := h1.2; rw [AE.tanh_ofReal, AE.sin_ofReal] at ha hb; have ha' : Real.sin (Real.tanh r) ≠ 1 := (by exact_mod_cast ha); have hb' : Real.sin (Real.tanh r) ≠ -1 := (by exact_mod_cast hb); refine ⟨⟨⟨by simp [AE.cosh_ofReal_ne_zero], by rw [AE.atanh.D_iff]; exact ⟨h0.2, h1.2⟩⟩, ⟨by simp [AE.cosh_ofReal_ne_zero], by rw [AE.tan.D_iff, ← AE.cos_eq]; exact h2.2⟩, by simp [AE.cosh_ofReal_ne_zero]⟩, ?_⟩; rw [AE.tanh_ofReal, AE.sin_ofReal, AE.tan_ofReal, AE.atanh_ofReal (lt_of_le_of_ne (Real.neg_one_le_sin _) hb'.symm) (lt_of_le_of_ne (Real.sin_le_one _) ha'), AE.asinh_ofReal, AE.artanh_sin_eq_arsinh_tan hy1 hy2]"))

Lean proofs (1; checked by lean-check, each under the statement it proves)

the claim
x ∈ ℂ
hd0x ∈ ℝ
h0sin(tanh(x)) ≠ 1
h1sin(tanh(x)) ≠ −1
h2nonzero(cos(tanh(x)))
⊢atanh(sin(tanh(x))) = asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
definedness side conditions (6, and 6 trivially true; t↓: t is defined)
h0tanh(x)↓
h0sin(tanh(x))↓ trivially true
h1tanh(x)↓
h1sin(tanh(x))↓ trivially true
h2tanh(x)↓
h2cos(tanh(x))↓ trivially true
h2nonzero(cos(tanh(x)))↓ trivially true
⊢tanh(x)↓ ×2
⊢sin(tanh(x))↓ trivially true
⊢atanh(sin(tanh(x)))↓
⊢tan(tanh(x))↓
⊢asinh(tan(tanh(x)))↓ trivially true
Lean statement
theorem case_sp_acj4_196_real (x : ℂ) (hd0 : AE.IsReal x) (h0 : ((AE.tanh.D x ∧ AE.sin.D (AE.tanh x)) ∧ (AE.sin (AE.tanh x) ≠ 1))) (h1 : ((AE.tanh.D x ∧ AE.sin.D (AE.tanh x)) ∧ (AE.sin (AE.tanh x) ≠ -1))) (h2 : (((AE.tanh.D x ∧ AE.cos.D (AE.tanh x)) ∧ AE.nonzero.D (AE.cos (AE.tanh x))) ∧ (AE.nonzero (AE.cos (AE.tanh x))))) : ((((AE.tanh.D x ∧ AE.sin.D (AE.tanh x)) ∧ AE.atanh.D (AE.sin (AE.tanh x))) ∧ (AE.tanh.D x ∧ AE.tan.D (AE.tanh x)) ∧ AE.asinh.D (AE.tan (AE.tanh x))) ∧ (AE.atanh (AE.sin (AE.tanh x)) = AE.asinh (AE.tan (AE.tanh x)))) :=
obtain ⟨r, rfl⟩ := AE.IsReal.exists hd0; have hy1 : -(Real.pi / 2) < Real.tanh r := (by linarith [Real.neg_one_lt_tanh r, Real.pi_gt_three]); have hy2 : Real.tanh r < Real.pi / 2 := (by linarith [Real.tanh_lt_one r, Real.pi_gt_three]); have ha := h0.2; have hb := h1.2; rw [AE.tanh_ofReal, AE.sin_ofReal] at ha hb; have ha' : Real.sin (Real.tanh r) ≠ 1 := (by exact_mod_cast ha); have hb' : Real.sin (Real.tanh r) ≠ -1 := (by exact_mod_cast hb); refine ⟨⟨⟨by simp [AE.cosh_ofReal_ne_zero], by rw [AE.atanh.D_iff]; exact ⟨h0.2, h1.2⟩⟩, ⟨by simp [AE.cosh_ofReal_ne_zero], by rw [AE.tan.D_iff, ← AE.cos_eq]; exact h2.2⟩, by simp [AE.cosh_ofReal_ne_zero]⟩, ?_⟩; rw [AE.tanh_ofReal, AE.sin_ofReal, AE.tan_ofReal, AE.atanh_ofReal (lt_of_le_of_ne (Real.neg_one_le_sin _) hb'.symm) (lt_of_le_of_ne (Real.sin_le_one _) ha'), AE.asinh_ofReal, AE.artanh_sin_eq_arsinh_tan hy1 hy2]

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.refine(Abs(W - 1) + sqrt(W**2), W > 2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

Machines: pi5 (indicative): it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise (Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled).

from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))
sataskQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))
sympy_extras.assumptions.simplifysympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(satsimp.refine(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, Q.positive(W), strict=True))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = simplify(satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum, strict=True))
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != 1)) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != (-1))) && ((Cos[Tanh[x]] != 0));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Sin[Tanh[x]]]) - (ArcSinh[Tan[Tanh[x]]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
from sympy.assumptions.satask import satask
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = satask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != 1)) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != (-1))) && ((Cos[Tanh[x]] != 0));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Sin[Tanh[x]]]) - (ArcSinh[Tan[Tanh[x]]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x')
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = (sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(W**2 - 2*W + 1 >= 0, W > 0), sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(log(W**2) - 2*log(W), 0), W > 0))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# the claim as a relation, sympy-extras' statement form (not Q.zero: see the adapter)
Answer = sympy_extras.assumptions.ask(Eq(Residual, 0), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x)))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import sympy_extras.assumptions
import sympy_extras.settings
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('sympy-extras')
# the commit sympy-extras was installed from (pip's direct_url.json): a VCS install's,
# or the one an https archive URL names; the ready line's version is
# CasVersion + ' (commit ' + Commit[:12] + ')', or ' (commit unknown)' without one
import json as Json, re as Regex
Source = Json.loads(importlib.metadata.distribution('sympy-extras').read_text('direct_url.json') or '{}')
Archive = Regex.match('https://[^#]*/archive/([0-9a-f]{40})[.](tar[.]gz|zip)(#|$)', Source.get('url', '')) if 'archive_info' in Source else None
Commit = Source.get('vcs_info', {}).get('commit_id') or (Archive and Archive.group(1))
# its settings: every time limit of the package is multiplied by time_scale
Settings = f'{sympy_extras.settings.settings!r}, time_scale={sympy_extras.settings.settings.time_scale}'
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(log(W) + log(exp(W)) + sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2, W > 0)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = sympy_extras.assumptions.simplify(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satsimp
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satsimp')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satsimp.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = satsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))
satsimp.refinesatsimp.refine(Residual, Assum)→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))
answernull
sympy-simplify 1.14.0no verdictnulldifference → -asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x))) · simplify → -asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))0.073 pi5 · indicative2026-10-18 21:29:48 ba9dc4def822Q.real(x) ∧ Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1) ∧ Ne(sin(tanh(x)), (-1)) ∧ (~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x)))) ⊢ Eq(atanh(sin(tanh(x))), asinh(tan(tanh(x))))null

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = simplify(sin(W)**2 + cos(W)**2)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x)))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = simplify(Residual)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
differenceatanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))
askQ.zero(Residual)→None
answernull

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x)))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

autoResidual→-asinh(tan(tanh(x))) + atanh(sin(tanh(x)))

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)
(* evaluated in a forked copy of the setup kernel: TimeConstrained would not fire here (no timer thread), so the parent enforces a hard timeout of 9 s by killing this process *)
(
  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != 1)) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != (-1))) && ((Cos[Tanh[x]] != 0));
  Residual = (ArcTanh[Sin[Tanh[x]]]) - (ArcSinh[Tan[Tanh[x]]]);
  Result = FullSimplify[Residual, Assum];
  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

import sympy

CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:

print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))

Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
import satrefine
import importlib.metadata
CasVersion = importlib.metadata.version('satrefine')
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = satrefine.refine(Abs(W), Q.positive(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
Result = satrefine.refine(Residual, Assum)
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

import sympy

W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')

setup — not timed (imports, warm-up, cache clearing: run once per start)
(
  $HistoryLength = 0;
  $Messages = {};
  Warmup = FullSimplify[Sin[W]^2 + Cos[W]^2, Element[W, Reals]];
  GenericWarmup = Quiet[{Simplify[Abs[W] + W, W < 0], Simplify[Sqrt[W^2] - W, W > 0], Refine[Sqrt[W^2], W > 0], Simplify[Log[W V] - Log[W] - Log[V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[Log[-W] - Log[W] - I Pi, W < 0], Simplify[(-1)^(2 W) - 1, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Mod[W + 1, 2], Element[W, Integers] && Element[(W - 1)/2, Integers]], Simplify[ArcTan[W] + ArcTan[1/W] - Pi/2, W > 0], FullSimplify[ArcCot[W] - ArcTan[1/W], W > 0], Simplify[Abs[Exp[I W]] - 1, Element[W, Reals]], Simplify[Re[W] - (W + Conjugate[W])/2], Simplify[Gamma[W + 1] - W!, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0], Simplify[Floor[W] - W, Element[W, Integers]], Simplify[Sqrt[W] Sqrt[V] - Sqrt[W V], W > 0 && V > 0], Simplify[HeavisideTheta[W] - 1, W > 0], Simplify[2^W 2^V - 2^(W + V), Element[W, Reals] && Element[V, Reals]], Simplify[Sum[V, {V, 1, W}] - W (W + 1)/2, Element[W, Integers] && W > 0]}];
  Quiet[Check[Message[General::argx, "W", 0], Null]];
  {$Version, Warmup, Length[GenericWarmup]}
)

  Assum = (Element[x, Reals]) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != 1)) && ((Sin[Tanh[x]] != (-1))) && ((Cos[Tanh[x]] != 0));

  Answer = Which[Result === 0, True, NumericQ[Result] && TrueQ[Result != 0], False, True, Null];
  {Residual, Result, Answer}
)

import sympy

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False)

import sympy

W = Symbol('W')

# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))

print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = ask(Q.positive(W**2 + 1), Q.real(W))
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', real=True)
Assum = And(Q.real(x), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), 1, evaluate=False), Ne(sin(tanh(x)), -1, evaluate=False), ~Q.zero(cos(tanh(x))))
Residual = atanh(sin(tanh(x))) - asinh(tan(tanh(x)))
# not Q.eq: in SymPy 1.14 ask(Q.eq(x**2, x), Q.real(x)) is wrongly False
Answer = ask(Q.zero(Residual), Assum)
print(Residual, Answer, sep='\n')
